# This file is how we call the story cycle dragon variables from the ruin character to give dragons their genes
# Each svalue (scripted value) is split into two IF statements, one for the Dragonrider poses (identifed by flag currently riding)
# the other If statement is for retaining dragon appearances when they die
# If new dragon genes are added, you have to add the appropriate svalue in the format everything else uses below
# You then call the 'gene_name_svalue' in the portrait modifier value slot!

# Template
# GENE_NAME_HERE_svalue = {
# 	value = {
# 		if = {
# 			limit = {
# 				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
# 				title:c_ruins.holder = {
# 					any_owned_story = {
# 						story_type = story_dragon_variable_storage
# 					}
# 				}
# 			}
# 			title:c_ruins.holder = {
# 				every_owned_story = {
# 					limit = {
# 						story_type = story_dragon_variable_storage
# 						var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
# 					}
# 					save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
# 				}
# 			}
# 			value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:GENE_NAME_HERE_svalue
# 		}
# 		if = {
# 			limit = {
# 				title:c_ruins.holder = {
# 					any_owned_story = {
# 						story_type = story_dragon_variable_storage
# 					}
# 				}
# 			}
# 			title:c_ruins.holder = {
# 				every_owned_story = {
# 					limit = {
# 						story_type = story_dragon_variable_storage
# 						var:dragon_id ?= root
# 					}
# 					save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
# 				}
# 			}
# 			value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:GENE_NAME_HERE_svalue
# 		}
# 	}
# }

dragon_age_svalue = {
	value = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = dragon_age }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:dragon_age
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = dragon_age }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:dragon_age
			}
		}
	}
}

dragon_size_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: размер для мёртвого дракона, у которого данных нет нигде.
		# Переменные на мёртвом персонаже CK3 не сохраняет, поэтому размер
		# восстанавливается вычислением: повторяем кривую роста AGOT из
		# agot_back_apply_dragon_aging_effect (3/год до 11 лет, 2/год до 15,
		# дальше 1/год) и применяем множители телосложения, как в dragon_size.
		# Первый вариант не делит: ниже отработает ветка хранилища и поделит сама.
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = no
				NOT = { has_variable = dragon_size }
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			value = 0
			if = {
				limit = { age >= 15 }
				add = 27
				add = age
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { age >= 11 }
				add = 13
				add = { value = age multiply = 2 }
			}
			else = {
				add = { value = age add = 1 multiply = 3 }
			}
			if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon } add = dragon_base_dragon_size_value }
			if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_good_3 } multiply = dragon_physique_good_3_size_mult }
			else_if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_good_2 } multiply = dragon_physique_good_2_size_mult }
			else_if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_good_1 } multiply = dragon_physique_good_1_size_mult }
			else_if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_bad_1 } multiply = dragon_physique_bad_1_size_mult }
			else_if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_bad_2 } multiply = dragon_physique_bad_2_size_mult }
			else_if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_bad_3 } multiply = dragon_physique_bad_3_size_mult }
			if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_spindly } multiply = dragon_spindly_size_mult }
		}
		# Второй вариант — партия без системы хранилища: делить некому, делим тут.
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_alive = no
				NOT = { has_variable = dragon_size }
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = no
			}
			value = 0
			if = {
				limit = { age >= 15 }
				add = 27
				add = age
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { age >= 11 }
				add = 13
				add = { value = age multiply = 2 }
			}
			else = {
				add = { value = age add = 1 multiply = 3 }
			}
			if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon } add = dragon_base_dragon_size_value }
			if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_good_3 } multiply = dragon_physique_good_3_size_mult }
			else_if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_good_2 } multiply = dragon_physique_good_2_size_mult }
			else_if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_good_1 } multiply = dragon_physique_good_1_size_mult }
			else_if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_bad_1 } multiply = dragon_physique_bad_1_size_mult }
			else_if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_bad_2 } multiply = dragon_physique_bad_2_size_mult }
			else_if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_physique_bad_3 } multiply = dragon_physique_bad_3_size_mult }
			if = { limit = { has_trait = dragon_spindly } multiply = dragon_spindly_size_mult }
			divide = 300
			max = 1
		}
		# MDE fix: запасной путь для драконов БЕЗ записи в gl_dragon_variable_storage.
		# Такие записи не создаются для драконов, умерших до начала партии, — у них
		# обе ветки ниже не находят ничего, значение остаётся 0, делится на 300 и даёт
		# минимальный масштаб: крошечный бесцветный дракон вместо Балериона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ и намеренно не делит: если хранилище найдётся, оно
		# перезапишет значение своим и поделит само. Второй вариант — на случай
		# партии вообще без системы хранилища, там делить некому.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = dragon_size
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			value = var:dragon_size
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = dragon_size
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = no
			}
			value = var:dragon_size
			divide = 300
			max = 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = dragon_size }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:dragon_size
			}
			divide = 300
			max = 1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = dragon_size }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:dragon_size
			}
			divide = 300
			max = 1
		}
	}
}

gene_dragonrider_size_svalue = {
	value = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = dragon_age }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:dragon_age
			}
			divide = 300
			max = 1
		}
	}
}

gene_dragon_primary_color_value_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Цветовой ген: номер общий для ВСЕХ цветовых генов, поэтому дракон
			# получает цельную палитру одного канонного дракона, а не смесь
			# оттенка от одного и насыщенности от другого — иначе выходили
			# ядовитые сочетания, которых в каноне нет.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 0 } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 1 } value = 0.18 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 2 } value = 0.48 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 3 } value = 0.482 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 4 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 5 } value = 0.508 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 6 } value = 0.51 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 7 } value = 0.53 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 8 } value = 0.53 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 9 } value = 0.532 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 10 } value = 0.536 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 11 } value = 0.55 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 12 } value = 0.552 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 13 } value = 0.554 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 14 } value = 0.572 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 15 } value = 0.574 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 16 } value = 0.574 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 17 } value = 0.576 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 18 } value = 0.576 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 19 } value = 0.576 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 20 } value = 0.58 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 21 } value = 0.582 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 22 } value = 0.584 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 23 } value = 0.584 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 24 } value = 0.598 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 25 } value = 0.598 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 26 } value = 0.6 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 27 } value = 0.604 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 28 } value = 0.606 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 29 } value = 0.608 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 30 } value = 0.61 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 31 } value = 0.614 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 32 } value = 0.62 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 33 } value = 0.626 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 34 } value = 0.63 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 35 } value = 0.63 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 36 } value = 0.69 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 37 } value = 0.698 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 38 } value = 0.71 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 39 } value = 0.98 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 40 } value = 0.98 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 41 } value = 0.98 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 42 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 0.608 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 0.536 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_arrax  this = character:dragon_arrax } value = 0.48 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_balerion  this = character:dragon_balerion } value = 0.98 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_cannibal  this = character:dragon_cannibal } value = 0.98 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_caraxes  this = character:dragon_caraxes } value = 0.53 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_dreamfyre  this = character:dragon_dreamfyre } value = 0.55 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_drogon  this = character:dragon_drogon } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_essovius  this = character:dragon_essovius } value = 0.62 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_ghiscar  this = character:dragon_ghiscar } value = 0.63 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_grey_ghost  this = character:dragon_grey_ghost } value = 0.552 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meleys  this = character:dragon_meleys } value = 0.63 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meraxes  this = character:dragon_meraxes } value = 0.53 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_moondancer  this = character:dragon_moondancer } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morghul  this = character:dragon_morghul } value = 0.98 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morning  this = character:dragon_morning } value = 0.598 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nagga  this = character:dragon_nagga } value = 0.482 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_neltharion  this = character:dragon_neltharion } value = 0.18 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nessarion  this = character:dragon_nessarion } value = 0.604 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nightfyre  this = character:dragon_nightfyre } value = 0.626 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_pythax  this = character:dragon_pythax } value = 0.51 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_quicksilver  this = character:dragon_quicksilver } value = 0.576 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rhaegal  this = character:dragon_rhaegal } value = 0.61 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rovax  this = character:dragon_rovax } value = 0.698 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_seasmoke  this = character:dragon_seasmoke } value = 0.532 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.71 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.598 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0.576 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.572 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0.58 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.574 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.508 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0.614 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.584 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.606 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.6 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.574 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.576 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.582 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.69 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.584 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.554 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_primary_color_value }
			value = var:gene_dragon_primary_color_value
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_primary_color_value }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_primary_color_value
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_primary_color_value }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_primary_color_value
			}
		}
	}
}

gene_dragon_secondary_hue_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Цветовой ген: номер общий для ВСЕХ цветовых генов, поэтому дракон
			# получает цельную палитру одного канонного дракона, а не смесь

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.548 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0.332 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.62 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.09 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.044 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0.08 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.69 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.392 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.95 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.006 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.02 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.03 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.34 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.822 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_secondary_hue }
			value = var:gene_dragon_secondary_hue
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_secondary_hue }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_secondary_hue
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_secondary_hue }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_secondary_hue
			}
		}
	}
}
gene_dragon_secondary_value_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.

			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Цветовой ген: номер общий для ВСЕХ цветовых генов, поэтому дракон
			# получает цельную палитру одного канонного дракона, а не смесь
			# оттенка от одного и насыщенности от другого — иначе выходили

gene_dragon_tertiary_hue_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Цветовой ген: номер общий для ВСЕХ цветовых генов, поэтому дракон
			# получает цельную палитру одного канонного дракона, а не смесь
			# оттенка от одного и насыщенности от другого — иначе выходили
			# ядовитые сочетания, которых в каноне нет.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 1 } value = 0.002 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 2 } value = 0.006 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 3 } value = 0.038 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 4 } value = 0.04 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 5 } value = 0.04 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 6 } value = 0.04 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 7 } value = 0.042 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 8 } value = 0.07 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 9 } value = 0.092 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 12 } value = 0.114 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 13 } value = 0.114 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 14 } value = 0.156 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 15 } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 16 } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 17 } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 18 } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 19 } value = 0.324 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 20 } value = 0.338 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 25 } value = 0.63 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 26 } value = 0.81 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 27 } value = 0.81 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 28 } value = 0.818 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 29 } value = 0.854 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 30 } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 31 } value = 0.9 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 37 } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 38 } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 39 } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 40 } value = 0.928 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 41 } value = 0.998 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 42 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 0.62 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 1 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nagga  this = character:dragon_nagga } value = 0.56 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_neltharion  this = character:dragon_neltharion } value = 0.928 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nessarion  this = character:dragon_nessarion } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nightfyre  this = character:dragon_nightfyre } value = 0.006 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_pythax  this = character:dragon_pythax } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_quicksilver  this = character:dragon_quicksilver } value = 0.63 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rhaegal  this = character:dragon_rhaegal } value = 0.324 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rovax  this = character:dragon_rovax } value = 0.156 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_seasmoke  this = character:dragon_seasmoke } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.04 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.818 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.04 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0.092 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.114 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.81 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.998 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.338 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.04 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.07 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.042 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.10 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_tertiary_hue }
			value = var:gene_dragon_tertiary_hue
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_tertiary_hue }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_tertiary_hue
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_tertiary_hue }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_tertiary_hue
			}
		}
	}
}

gene_dragon_eye_color_hue_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Цветовой ген: номер общий для ВСЕХ цветовых генов, поэтому дракон
			# получает цельную палитру одного канонного дракона, а не смесь
			# оттенка от одного и насыщенности от другого — иначе выходили
			# ядовитые сочетания, которых в каноне нет.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 0 } value = 0.006 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 1 } value = 0.008 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 2 } value = 0.036 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 3 } value = 0.038 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 4 } value = 0.038 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 5 } value = 0.038 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 6 } value = 0.042 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 7 } value = 0.046 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 8 } value = 0.048 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 10 } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 11 } value = 0.07 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 12 } value = 0.07 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 13 } value = 0.09 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 14 } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 15 } value = 0.1 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_seasmoke  this = character:dragon_seasmoke } value = 0.602 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.378 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.042 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.048 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0.628 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.65 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0.09 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.07 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.296 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0.652 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.702 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.13 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.046 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.038 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.036 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.038 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.07 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.278 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.1 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_eye_color_hue }
			value = var:gene_dragon_eye_color_hue
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_eye_color_hue }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_eye_color_hue
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_eye_color_hue }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_eye_color_hue
			}
		}
	}
}

gene_dragon_horn_color_hue_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Цветовой ген: номер общий для ВСЕХ цветовых генов, поэтому дракон
			# получает цельную палитру одного канонного дракона, а не смесь
			# оттенка от одного и насыщенности от другого — иначе выходили
			# ядовитые сочетания, которых в каноне нет.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 1 } value = 0.002 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 2 } value = 0.002 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 3 } value = 0.004 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 4 } value = 0.004 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 5 } value = 0.01 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 6 } value = 0.01 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 7 } value = 0.02 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 8 } value = 0.03 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 9 } value = 0.038 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 10 } value = 0.04 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 11 } value = 0.04 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 12 } value = 0.042 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 13 } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 14 } value = 0.066 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 15 } value = 0.07 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 16 } value = 0.08 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 17 } value = 0.11 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 18 } value = 0.12 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 19 } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 20 } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 21 } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 22 } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 23 } value = 0.336 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 24 } value = 0.35 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 25 } value = 0.38 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 26 } value = 0.38 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 27 } value = 0.438 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 28 } value = 0.53 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 29 } value = 0.548 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 30 } value = 0.596 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 31 } value = 0.61 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 32 } value = 0.614 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 34 } value = 0.622 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 35 } value = 0.632 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 37 } value = 0.754 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 38 } value = 0.754 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 39 } value = 0.816 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 40 } value = 0.828 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 41 } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 42 } value = 0.954 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 0.038 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_dreamfyre  this = character:dragon_dreamfyre } value = 0.53 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_drogon  this = character:dragon_drogon } value = 0.004 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_essovius  this = character:dragon_essovius } value = 0.438 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_ghiscar  this = character:dragon_ghiscar } value = 0.07 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_grey_ghost  this = character:dragon_grey_ghost } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meleys  this = character:dragon_meleys } value = 0.004 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meraxes  this = character:dragon_meraxes } value = 0.816 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_neltharion  this = character:dragon_neltharion } value = 0.02 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nessarion  this = character:dragon_nessarion } value = 0.622 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nightfyre  this = character:dragon_nightfyre } value = 0.67 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_pythax  this = character:dragon_pythax } value = 0.754 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rhaegal  this = character:dragon_rhaegal } value = 0.042 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rovax  this = character:dragon_rovax } value = 0.596 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_seasmoke  this = character:dragon_seasmoke } value = 0.01 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.04 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.336 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0.61 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.62 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0.08 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.12 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.03 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.754 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.35 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.002 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.38 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.01 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.04 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.828 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.066 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.11 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_horn_color_hue }
			value = var:gene_dragon_horn_color_hue
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_horn_color_hue }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_horn_color_hue
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_horn_color_hue }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_horn_color_hue
			}
		}
	}
}

gene_dragon_brow_width_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Ген формы: номер свой, формы можно смешивать без вреда.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 1 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 2 } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 3 } value = 0.06 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 4 } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 5 } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 6 } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 7 } value = 0.34 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 8 } value = 0.34 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 9 } value = 0.34 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 10 } value = 0.35 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 11 } value = 0.38 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 12 } value = 0.38 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 13 } value = 0.39 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 14 } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 15 } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 16 } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 17 } value = 0.41 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 18 } value = 0.41 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 19 } value = 0.47 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 20 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 21 } value = 0.52 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 22 } value = 0.67 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 23 } value = 0.7 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 24 } value = 0.76 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 25 } value = 0.83 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 26 } value = 0.85 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 27 } value = 0.86 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 28 } value = 0.89 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 29 } value = 0.89 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 30 } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 31 } value = 0.93 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 32 } value = 0.96 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 33 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 34 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 35 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 36 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 37 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 38 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 39 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 40 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 41 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_3_svalue = 42 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 1.0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_arrax  this = character:dragon_arrax } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_balerion  this = character:dragon_balerion } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_cannibal  this = character:dragon_cannibal } value = 0.41 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_caraxes  this = character:dragon_caraxes } value = 1.00 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_dreamfyre  this = character:dragon_dreamfyre } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_drogon  this = character:dragon_drogon } value = 0.34 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_essovius  this = character:dragon_essovius } value = 0.38 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_ghiscar  this = character:dragon_ghiscar } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_grey_ghost  this = character:dragon_grey_ghost } value = 1.0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meleys  this = character:dragon_meleys } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meraxes  this = character:dragon_meraxes } value = 0.96 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_moondancer  this = character:dragon_moondancer } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morghul  this = character:dragon_morghul } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morning  this = character:dragon_morning } value = 0.47 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nagga  this = character:dragon_nagga } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_neltharion  this = character:dragon_neltharion } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nessarion  this = character:dragon_nessarion } value = 1.0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nightfyre  this = character:dragon_nightfyre } value = 0.93 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_pythax  this = character:dragon_pythax } value = 0.35 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_quicksilver  this = character:dragon_quicksilver } value = 1.0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rhaegal  this = character:dragon_rhaegal } value = 0.34 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rovax  this = character:dragon_rovax } value = 0.85 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_seasmoke  this = character:dragon_seasmoke } value = 0.76 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.38 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.83 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.41 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.89 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0.7 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.89 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.39 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.86 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.06 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.52 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.67 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.34 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_brow_width }
			value = var:gene_dragon_brow_width
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_brow_width }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_brow_width
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_brow_width }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_brow_width
			}
		}
	}
}

	value = {

		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не

		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Ген формы: номер свой, формы можно смешивать без вреда.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 1 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 2 } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 3 } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 4 } value = 0.13 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 5 } value = 0.16 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 6 } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 7 } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 8 } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 9 } value = 0.22 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 10 } value = 0.27 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 11 } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 12 } value = 0.32 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 13 } value = 0.35 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 14 } value = 0.37 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 15 } value = 0.39 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 16 } value = 0.41 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 17 } value = 0.41 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 18 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 19 } value = 0.52 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 20 } value = 0.55 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 21 } value = 0.56 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 22 } value = 0.6 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 23 } value = 0.6 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 24 } value = 0.63 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 25 } value = 0.64 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 26 } value = 0.68 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 27 } value = 0.74 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 28 } value = 0.75 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 30 } value = 0.76 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 31 } value = 0.77 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 33 } value = 0.81 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 34 } value = 0.83 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 35 } value = 0.83 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 36 } value = 0.83 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_6_svalue = 42 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 1.0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 0.2 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rhaegal  this = character:dragon_rhaegal } value = 0.83 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rovax  this = character:dragon_rovax } value = 0.88 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_seasmoke  this = character:dragon_seasmoke } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.32 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.96 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.35 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.68 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.27 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.89 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0.16 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.6 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.55 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.63 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.13 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.6 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.8 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.81 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.22 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.83 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_chin_profile }
			value = var:gene_dragon_chin_profile
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_chin_profile }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_chin_profile
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_chin_profile }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_chin_profile
			}
		}
	}
}

gene_dragon_head_roundness_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.05 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.41 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.54 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.34 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.01 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.60 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.27 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.21 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.41 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.27 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.32 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_head_roundness }
			value = var:gene_dragon_head_roundness
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_head_roundness }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_head_roundness
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_head_roundness }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_head_roundness
			}
		}
	}
}

gene_dragon_main_horn_shape_template_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 7 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_arrax  this = character:dragon_arrax } value = 2 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_balerion  this = character:dragon_balerion } value = 2 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_cannibal  this = character:dragon_cannibal } value = 3 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_caraxes  this = character:dragon_caraxes } value = 10 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_dreamfyre  this = character:dragon_dreamfyre } value = 5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_drogon  this = character:dragon_drogon } value = 10 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_essovius  this = character:dragon_essovius } value = 9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_ghiscar  this = character:dragon_ghiscar } value = 3 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_grey_ghost  this = character:dragon_grey_ghost } value = 6 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meleys  this = character:dragon_meleys } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meraxes  this = character:dragon_meraxes } value = 8 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_moondancer  this = character:dragon_moondancer } value = 5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morghul  this = character:dragon_morghul } value = 2 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morning  this = character:dragon_morning } value = 5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nagga  this = character:dragon_nagga } value = 4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_neltharion  this = character:dragon_neltharion } value = 5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nessarion  this = character:dragon_nessarion } value = 4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nightfyre  this = character:dragon_nightfyre } value = 9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_pythax  this = character:dragon_pythax } value = 6 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_quicksilver  this = character:dragon_quicksilver } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rhaegal  this = character:dragon_rhaegal } value = 10 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rovax  this = character:dragon_rovax } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_seasmoke  this = character:dragon_seasmoke } value = 10 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 10 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 8 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 2 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 7 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 6 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 10 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 8 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 10 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_main_horn_shape_template }
			value = var:gene_dragon_main_horn_shape_template
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_main_horn_shape_template }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_main_horn_shape_template
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
				has_trait = dragon
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_main_horn_shape_template }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_main_horn_shape_template
			}
		}
	}
}

	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
