				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_body_shading_template }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_body_shading_template
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
				has_trait = dragon
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_body_shading_template }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_body_shading_template
			}
		}
	}
}
gene_dragon_body_pattern_template_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_arrax  this = character:dragon_arrax } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_balerion  this = character:dragon_balerion } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_cannibal  this = character:dragon_cannibal } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_caraxes  this = character:dragon_caraxes } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_dreamfyre  this = character:dragon_dreamfyre } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_drogon  this = character:dragon_drogon } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_essovius  this = character:dragon_essovius } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_ghiscar  this = character:dragon_ghiscar } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_grey_ghost  this = character:dragon_grey_ghost } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meleys  this = character:dragon_meleys } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meraxes  this = character:dragon_meraxes } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_moondancer  this = character:dragon_moondancer } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morghul  this = character:dragon_morghul } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morning  this = character:dragon_morning } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nagga  this = character:dragon_nagga } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_neltharion  this = character:dragon_neltharion } value = 7 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nessarion  this = character:dragon_nessarion } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nightfyre  this = character:dragon_nightfyre } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_pythax  this = character:dragon_pythax } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_quicksilver  this = character:dragon_quicksilver } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rhaegal  this = character:dragon_rhaegal } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rovax  this = character:dragon_rovax } value = 6 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_seasmoke  this = character:dragon_seasmoke } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 7 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 1 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_body_pattern_template }
			value = var:gene_dragon_body_pattern_template
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_body_pattern_template }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_body_pattern_template
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
				has_trait = dragon
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_body_pattern_template }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_body_pattern_template
			}
		}
	}
}
gene_dragon_body_shading_value_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Цветовой ген: номер общий для ВСЕХ цветовых генов, поэтому дракон
			# получает цельную палитру одного канонного дракона, а не смесь
			# оттенка от одного и насыщенности от другого — иначе выходили
			# ядовитые сочетания, которых в каноне нет.

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_body_pattern_value }
			value = var:gene_dragon_body_pattern_value
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_body_pattern_value }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_body_pattern_value
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_body_pattern_value }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_body_pattern_value
			}
		}
	}
}

gene_dragon_wings_shading_template_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.

			limit = { is_alive = no }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 12 }

gene_dragon_wings_shading_value_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Цветовой ген: номер общий для ВСЕХ цветовых генов, поэтому дракон
			# получает цельную палитру одного канонного дракона, а не смесь
			# оттенка от одного и насыщенности от другого — иначе выходили
			# ядовитые сочетания, которых в каноне нет.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.95588 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0.25001 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.67309 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.15 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0.6 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.25 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.8 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.5751 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.8 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.23781 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.75 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.61 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_wings_shading_value }
			value = var:gene_dragon_wings_shading_value
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_wings_shading_value }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_wings_shading_value
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_wings_shading_value }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_wings_shading_value
			}
		}
	}
}

gene_dragon_jaw_width_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.

			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Ген формы: номер свой, формы можно смешивать без вреда.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0.6 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.69 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.49 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0.57 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.47 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.71 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.67 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.98 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.58 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.95 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.44 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.69 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.58 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_lower_jaw_height }
			value = var:gene_dragon_lower_jaw_height
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_lower_jaw_height }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_lower_jaw_height
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_lower_jaw_height }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_lower_jaw_height
			}
		}
	}
}
gene_dragon_lower_jaw_width_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.

			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Ген формы: номер свой, формы можно смешивать без вреда.

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.38 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.01 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.6 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.12 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.01 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_old_neck }
			value = var:gene_dragon_old_neck
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_old_neck }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_old_neck
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_old_neck }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_old_neck
			}
		}
	}
}

		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.76 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.57 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.55 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.51 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.97 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.42 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_snout_height }
			value = var:gene_dragon_snout_height
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_snout_height }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_snout_height
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_snout_height }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_snout_height
			}
		}
	}
}

	value = {

		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.17 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.34 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.04 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_snout_profile }
			value = var:gene_dragon_snout_profile
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_snout_profile }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_snout_profile
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_snout_profile }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_snout_profile
			}
		}
	}
}
gene_dragon_snout_width_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока

			limit = { is_alive = no }

			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Ген формы: номер свой, формы можно смешивать без вреда.

		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_upper_jaw_width }
			value = var:gene_dragon_upper_jaw_width
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_upper_jaw_width }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_upper_jaw_width
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_upper_jaw_width }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_upper_jaw_width
			}
		}
	}
}
gene_dragon_center_fin_size_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.

				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_back_spike_size }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_back_spike_size
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_back_spike_size }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_back_spike_size
			}
		}
	}
}
gene_dragon_horns_eyebrow_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.

			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Ген формы: номер свой, формы можно смешивать без вреда.

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.62 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.63 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.6 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.35 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.62 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.23 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.72 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.92 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.8 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_horns_eyebrow }
			value = var:gene_dragon_horns_eyebrow

					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val

				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage

				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_neck_spike_size }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_neck_spike_size
			}
		}
	}
}
gene_dragon_side_fin_size_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Ген формы: номер свой, формы можно смешивать без вреда.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_36_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_36_svalue = 1 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_36_svalue = 2 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_36_svalue = 3 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_36_svalue = 4 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_36_svalue = 5 } value = 0.02 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.46 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.02 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.46 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.28 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.97 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0.16 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.59 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.02 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.27 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.59 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.33 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.29 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.42 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_side_fin_size }
			value = var:gene_dragon_side_fin_size
		}

			limit = {

			}

				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage

					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon

				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_side_fin_size }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_side_fin_size

			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}

				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_side_fin_size }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_side_fin_size
			}
		}
	}

gene_dragon_neck_length_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Ген формы: номер свой, формы можно смешивать без вреда.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 1 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 2 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 3 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 4 } value = 0.06 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 5 } value = 0.19 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 6 } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 7 } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 8 } value = 0.21 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 9 } value = 0.22 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 10 } value = 0.23 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 11 } value = 0.26 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 12 } value = 0.26 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 13 } value = 0.27 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 14 } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 15 } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 16 } value = 0.32 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 17 } value = 0.33 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 18 } value = 0.35 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 19 } value = 0.39 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 20 } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 21 } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 22 } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 23 } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 24 } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 25 } value = 0.41 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 26 } value = 0.42 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 27 } value = 0.43 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 28 } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 29 } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 30 } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 31 } value = 0.46 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 32 } value = 0.47 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 33 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 34 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 35 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 36 } value = 0.58 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 37 } value = 0.62 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 38 } value = 0.7 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 39 } value = 0.7 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 40 } value = 0.85 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 41 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_24_svalue = 42 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 0.0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_arrax  this = character:dragon_arrax } value = 0.26 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_balerion  this = character:dragon_balerion } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_cannibal  this = character:dragon_cannibal } value = 0.58 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_caraxes  this = character:dragon_caraxes } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_dreamfyre  this = character:dragon_dreamfyre } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_drogon  this = character:dragon_drogon } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_essovius  this = character:dragon_essovius } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_ghiscar  this = character:dragon_ghiscar } value = 0.39 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_grey_ghost  this = character:dragon_grey_ghost } value = 0.46 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meleys  this = character:dragon_meleys } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meraxes  this = character:dragon_meraxes } value = 0.43 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_moondancer  this = character:dragon_moondancer } value = 0.21 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morghul  this = character:dragon_morghul } value = 0.35 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morning  this = character:dragon_morning } value = 0.06 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nagga  this = character:dragon_nagga } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_neltharion  this = character:dragon_neltharion } value = 0.42 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nessarion  this = character:dragon_nessarion } value = 0.7 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nightfyre  this = character:dragon_nightfyre } value = 0.33 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_pythax  this = character:dragon_pythax } value = 0.22 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_quicksilver  this = character:dragon_quicksilver } value = 0.0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rhaegal  this = character:dragon_rhaegal } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rovax  this = character:dragon_rovax } value = 0.27 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_seasmoke  this = character:dragon_seasmoke } value = 0.32 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.7 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0.85 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.41 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.00 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.26 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.23 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.62 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.19 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.47 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0.4 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_neck_length }
			value = var:gene_dragon_neck_length
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_neck_length }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_neck_length
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_neck_length }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_neck_length
			}
		}
	}
}
gene_dragon_metallic_scales_strength_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Цветовой ген: номер общий для ВСЕХ цветовых генов, поэтому дракон
			# получает цельную палитру одного канонного дракона, а не смесь
			# оттенка от одного и насыщенности от другого — иначе выходили
			# ядовитые сочетания, которых в каноне нет.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 1 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 2 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 3 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 4 } value = 0 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 17 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 18 } value = 0 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.0 }

		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.

			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_metallic_scales_strength }
			value = var:gene_dragon_metallic_scales_strength

		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes

			every_in_global_list = {

				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes

					var:dragon_id ?= root

gene_dragon_scale_shadows_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Цветовой ген: номер общий для ВСЕХ цветовых генов, поэтому дракон
			# получает цельную палитру одного канонного дракона, а не смесь
			# оттенка от одного и насыщенности от другого — иначе выходили
			# ядовитые сочетания, которых в каноне нет.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 1 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 2 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 3 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 4 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 5 } value = 0.01 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 6 } value = 0.15 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 7 } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 8 } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 9 } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 10 } value = 0.22 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 11 } value = 0.22 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 12 } value = 0.25 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 13 } value = 0.26 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 14 } value = 0.26 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 15 } value = 0.28 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 16 } value = 0.32 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 17 } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 18 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 19 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 20 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 21 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 22 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 23 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 24 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 25 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 26 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 27 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 28 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 29 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 30 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 31 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 32 } value = 0.5 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 34 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 35 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 36 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 37 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 38 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 39 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 40 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 41 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 42 } value = 0.8 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 0.22 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_arrax  this = character:dragon_arrax } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_balerion  this = character:dragon_balerion } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_cannibal  this = character:dragon_cannibal } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_caraxes  this = character:dragon_caraxes } value = 0.4 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_dreamfyre  this = character:dragon_dreamfyre } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_drogon  this = character:dragon_drogon } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_essovius  this = character:dragon_essovius } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_ghiscar  this = character:dragon_ghiscar } value = 0.01 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_grey_ghost  this = character:dragon_grey_ghost } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meleys  this = character:dragon_meleys } value = 0.32 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meraxes  this = character:dragon_meraxes } value = 0.26 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morghul  this = character:dragon_morghul } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morning  this = character:dragon_morning } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nagga  this = character:dragon_nagga } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_neltharion  this = character:dragon_neltharion } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nessarion  this = character:dragon_nessarion } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nightfyre  this = character:dragon_nightfyre } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_pythax  this = character:dragon_pythax } value = 0.28 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_quicksilver  this = character:dragon_quicksilver } value = 0 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rovax  this = character:dragon_rovax } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_seasmoke  this = character:dragon_seasmoke } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0.26 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0.25 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0.2 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.8 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.22 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_scale_shadows }
			value = var:gene_dragon_scale_shadows
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_scale_shadows }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_scale_shadows
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_scale_shadows }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_scale_shadows
			}
		}
	}
}

gene_dragon_wounded_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока
		# сравнениями за каждую отрисовку живого дракона.
		if = {
			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Ген формы: номер свой, формы можно смешивать без вреда.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 1 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 2 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 3 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 4 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 5 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 6 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 7 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 8 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 9 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 10 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 11 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 12 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 13 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 14 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 15 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 16 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 17 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 18 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 19 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 20 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 21 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 22 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 23 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 24 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 25 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 26 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 27 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 28 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 29 } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 30 } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 31 } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 32 } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 33 } value = 0.12 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 34 } value = 0.15 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 35 } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 36 } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 37 } value = 0.35 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 38 } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 39 } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 40 } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 41 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_48_svalue = 42 } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_arrax  this = character:dragon_arrax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_balerion  this = character:dragon_balerion } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_cannibal  this = character:dragon_cannibal } value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_caraxes  this = character:dragon_caraxes } value = 0.3 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_dreamfyre  this = character:dragon_dreamfyre } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_drogon  this = character:dragon_drogon } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_essovius  this = character:dragon_essovius } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_ghiscar  this = character:dragon_ghiscar } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_grey_ghost  this = character:dragon_grey_ghost } value = 0.0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meleys  this = character:dragon_meleys } value = 0.35 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_meraxes  this = character:dragon_meraxes } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_moondancer  this = character:dragon_moondancer } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morghul  this = character:dragon_morghul } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_morning  this = character:dragon_morning } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nagga  this = character:dragon_nagga } value = 0.12 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_neltharion  this = character:dragon_neltharion } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nessarion  this = character:dragon_nessarion } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_nightfyre  this = character:dragon_nightfyre } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_pythax  this = character:dragon_pythax } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_quicksilver  this = character:dragon_quicksilver } value = 0.1 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rhaegal  this = character:dragon_rhaegal } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_rovax  this = character:dragon_rovax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_seasmoke  this = character:dragon_seasmoke } value = 0.0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_serporoth  this = character:dragon_serporoth } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sheepstealer  this = character:dragon_sheepstealer } value = 0.9 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_shrykos  this = character:dragon_shrykos } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_silverwing  this = character:dragon_silverwing } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_stormcloud  this = character:dragon_stormcloud } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_sunfyre  this = character:dragon_sunfyre } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_syrax  this = character:dragon_syrax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_terrax  this = character:dragon_terrax } value = 0.0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tessarion  this = character:dragon_tessarion } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_tyraxes  this = character:dragon_tyraxes } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_urrax  this = character:dragon_urrax } value = 0.0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_valryon  this = character:dragon_valryon } value = 0.5 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vassarion  this = character:dragon_vassarion } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermax  this = character:dragon_vermax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithor  this = character:dragon_vermithor } value = 0.45 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vermithrax  this = character:dragon_vermithrax } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_vhagar  this = character:dragon_vhagar } value = 0.15 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_viserion  this = character:dragon_viserion } value = 0 }
		}
		# Fallback: the dragon's own spawn variables. Lowest priority - the storage
		# branches below overwrite it once the dragon storage list exists. Without this,
		# portraits rendered before the storage populates get 0 and the DNA baseline
		# shows instead of the scripted appearance.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = gene_dragon_wounded }
			value = var:gene_dragon_wounded
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_character_flag = currently_riding_dragon
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root.var:current_dragon
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_wounded }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_wounded
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				agot_has_dragon_storage_system_global_list = yes
			}
			every_in_global_list = {
				variable = gl_dragon_variable_storage
				limit = {
					var:dragon_id ?= root
				}
				save_temporary_scope_as = dragon_var_story_val
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = scope:dragon_var_story_val
					scope:dragon_var_story_val = { has_variable = gene_dragon_wounded }
				}
				value = scope:dragon_var_story_val.var:gene_dragon_wounded
			}
		}
	}
}

gene_dragon_primary_color_hue_svalue = {
	value = {
		# MDE fix: канонные значения для мёртвых драконов, у которых данных нет
		# ни на персонаже, ни в записи хранилища. У мёртвого персонажа CK3 не
		# сохраняет переменные вообще, поэтому записать их некуда — значение
		# приходится задавать прямо здесь, по личности дракона.
		# Ветка стоит ПЕРВОЙ: любая настоящая запись ниже её перезапишет.
		# Гейт is_alive = no держит живых в стороне, чтобы не платить сорока

			limit = { is_alive = no }
			# MDE fix: неканонный мёртвый дракон — данных нет нигде, восстанавливать
			# нечего. Значение берётся из настоящего списка канонных значений этого
			# гена, номер выбирается по навыкам дракона: они переживают смерть.
			# Цветовой ген: номер общий для ВСЕХ цветовых генов, поэтому дракон
			# получает цельную палитру одного канонного дракона, а не смесь
			# оттенка от одного и насыщенности от другого — иначе выходили
			# ядовитые сочетания, которых в каноне нет.
			# Канонные ветки ниже перезаписывают это точными значениями.
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 0 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 1 } value = 0 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 2 } value = 0.01 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 3 } value = 0.01 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 4 } value = 0.03 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 13 } value = 0.13 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 14 } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 15 } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 16 } value = 0.24 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 17 } value = 0.24 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 27 } value = 0.51 }

			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 41 } value = 0.962 }
			if = { limit = { mde_dead_pick_color_svalue = 42 } value = 0.97 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_anogaros  this = character:dragon_anogaros } value = 0.102 }
			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_archonei  this = character:dragon_archonei } value = 0 }

			if = { limit = { exists = character:dragon_essovius  this = character:dragon_essovius } value = 0.76 }
